| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 | 57 | \| Annual gross rents from unfurnished letting, whole tax household, must not exceed \| EUR 15,000 \| "revenu brut foncier annuel n’excède pas 15 000 €"; the Brochure chapters print it for rents received in 2025 and the pages read print no different limit for rents of 2026 \| | Exact retained current LF59 / 3.1 Micro-foncier |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 | 58 | \| Flat allowance, applied automatically \| 30% \| "l’application d’un abattement de 30 %"; it stands for every charge \| | Exact retained current LF60, 3.1 Micro-foncier |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 | 60 | - **Who and when.** Micro-foncier applies by right (de plein droit) to a household whose total gross rents from unfurnished letting in the year do not exceed the limit in the table above, and which does not let property under one of the special regimes listed below (BOFiP, linked above, § 20). The 2026 Brochure pratique, chapter on revenus fonciers (linked in Sources), says the limit is tested on rents received in the year, "charges non comprises", "quelle que soit la durée de la location", and is not applied pro rata. | Exact retained current LF62 / 3.1 Micro-foncier |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 | 61 | - **Cliff, not allowance.** If gross rents exceed the limit, the whole of the rental income goes to the régime réel; there is no partial micro regime. | Exact retained current LF63, 3.1 Micro-foncier |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 | 62 | - **How to declare.** Enter gross rents on form 2042, box 4BE. No form 2044 is filed. No deficit can arise under micro-foncier, because the flat allowance replaces every actual charge. | Exact retained current LF64 / 3.1 Micro-foncier |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 | 63 | - **Who is excluded (BOFiP § 60 and § 70).** A household is outside micro-foncier when any member owns a classified historic monument (monument historique) whose deficit is set against global income; or a dwelling for which one of the art. 31 deductions is claimed (Périssol, Besson neuf, Robien, Borloo neuf, Besson ancien, Robien ZRR, Borloo ancien, Cosse); or property under the art. 199 decies EA or 199 decies F tourism reductions or the Loc'Avantages reduction (art. 199 tricies); or certain shares (parts of a non-transparent company letting such property, Robien SCPI or Borloo SCPI parts). A holder of SCI parts or FPI units who owns no directly let unfurnished property is also outside micro-foncier and reports the share of net income directly on form 2042. | Exact retained current LF65, 3.1 Micro-foncier |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 | 64 | - **Who is NOT excluded.** BOFiP § 60 says the Scellier, Duflot, Pinel, Denormandie and Malraux income tax reductions do not prevent micro-foncier. Partners of an art. 8 SCI letting bare property can use micro-foncier only if they also own at least one property let unfurnished directly (BOFiP § 50). | Exact retained current LF66, 3.1 Micro-foncier |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 | 68 | - **Mandatory or by option.** The régime réel applies when gross rents exceed the micro-foncier limit, or when a household within the limit opts for it by filing a form 2044. The 2026 Brochure pratique, chapter on revenus fonciers, says "Cette option est irrévocable pendant trois ans"; after those three years, each 2044 filed counts as the option for that year only. | Exact retained current LF70, 3.2 Régime réel |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 | 69 | - **Forms.** Net income or deficit is computed on form 2044 or form 2044 spéciale, then carried to form 2042, box 4BA and the boxes after it. The 2044 spéciale is needed for the special regimes (2026 Brochure pratique, chapter on form 2044, linked in Sources). | Exact retained current LF71, 3.2 Régime réel |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 | 71 | **Deductible charges (2026 Brochure pratique, chapter "Déclaration des revenus fonciers no 2044")** | Exact retained current LF73, 3.2 Régime réel |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 | 76 | \| Repair, maintenance and improvement works (travaux de réparation, d'entretien et d'amélioration) \| Yes \| Not if they affect "la structure de l’immeuble": construction, reconstruction and extension works are not deductible \| | FR-A12 current3.2expensegates: residentialimprovement,commercialexceptions and31Decemberdeparture-year cutoff. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 | 77 | \| Taxe foncière \| Yes, unless the lease puts it on the tenant \| The household waste tax (TEOM) on the same notice is not deductible: it is a charge paid for the tenant and recovered from them \| | Exact retained current LF79, 3.2 Régime réel |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 | 80 | \| Co-ownership provisions voted by the general meeting and paid to the syndic \| Yes, in the year paid \| The unused part is added back in the following year when the accounts are approved \| | Exact retained current LF82 / 3.2 Régime réel |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 | 81 | \| Charges the landlord should have recovered from the tenant (charges locatives) \| No, as a rule \| Exception: charges not recovered when the tenant leaves \| | FR-A12 current3.2expensegates: residentialimprovement,commercialexceptions and31Decemberdeparture-year cutoff. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 | 83 | - **Construction, reconstruction and extension works are not deductible** from rental income. The Brochure chapter on form 2044 says these works must not affect "la structure de l’immeuble". | Exact retained current LF85, 3.2 Régime réel |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 | 99 | \| Raised yearly limit where the deficit comes from energy renovation works moving a dwelling from class E, F or G to class A, B, C or D \| EUR 21,400 \| "global à hauteur de 21 400 € en cas de dépenses de rénovation"; the chapter on form 2044 ties it to works with a quote accepted from 5.11.2022 and paid from 1.1.2023 to 31.12.2025, and the nouveautés page says the limit is extended (prorogée) to 31.12.2027 (loi de finances 2026, art. 47) \| | Exact retained current LF101, 3.3 Déficit foncier (rental loss) |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 | 101 | - **Which part goes where.** Only the part of the deficit made up of charges other than loan interest goes against global income, up to the limit in the first table above. The surplus over that limit, and the whole part that comes from loan interest, can be set only against rental income of the following 10 years (chapter on form 2044, deficits section). | Exact retained current LF103 / 3.3 Déficit foncier (rental loss) |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 | 102 | - **The letting condition.** Setting a deficit against global income is conditional on the property staying let until 31 December of the third year after that set-off. If the property is sold, or the letting stops, within that period, the rental income and global income of the years concerned are recomputed without the set-off. The chapter on form 2044 names the exceptions: dismissal, invalidity or death of the taxpayer or of the spouse, and expropriation of the property. | Exact retained current LF104, 3.3 Déficit foncier (rental loss) |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 | 103 | - **Raised limit, conditions.** The raised limit in the second table above needs proof of the energy class before the works (class E, F or G) and after them (class A, B, C or D). For works paid in 2026 or 2027, check the conditions of the extension with the impots.gouv.fr guidance for 2026 income before relying on it; the 2026 Brochure chapter describes the conditions as they stood for works paid by 31.12.2025. | Exact retained current LF105, 3.3 Déficit foncier (rental loss) |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 | 104 | - **Planning.** Bunching large works into one year to use the global income set-off is a lawful choice; this skill does not recommend it without the client's full picture. See `france-tax-optimization`. | Exact retained current LF106, 3.3 Déficit foncier (rental loss) |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 | 105 | - **New for 2026: a depreciation deduction for bare lets.** The nouveautés page says the 2026 finance law (art. 47, CGI art. 31) created a deduction of depreciation on part of the purchase price of a dwelling acquired for bare letting, with rent and tenant income ceilings and a nine-year letting commitment, for acquisitions or building permit requests between 21.2.2026 and 31.12.2028. This skill does not cover it: refer (see "When to refuse or refer"). | Exact retained current LF107, 3.3 Déficit foncier (rental loss) |

| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 | 145 | - **The test, and the two-year rule.** The impots.gouv.fr page says the micro regime applies when the receipts of the previous year "ou de l'avant-dernière année" do not exceed the limit. F23267 says crossing a limit in one year only does not end the regime: income of year N leaves micro-BIC only if the limit was exceeded in both N-1 and N-2. The allowances in the first table above apply from income of 2025 (nouveautés page: "À compter du 1.1.2025"); F23267 prints no different rate for 2026. | Exact retained current LF147 / 4.1 Micro-BIC |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 | 146 | - **Long-term furnished lets in 2026.** F23267 puts long-term furnished letting under its services tab: the micro regime applies to income received in 2026 if turnover in 2025 or in 2024 did not exceed the limit in the second table above. | Exact retained current LF148, 4.1 Micro-BIC |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 | 147 | - **2042-C-PRO boxes (LMNP, micro).** The impots.gouv.fr page lists them: 5NG, 5OG or 5PG for chambres d'hôtes and classified tourist lets; 5NH, 5OH, 5PH for unclassified tourist lets; 5NI, 5OI or 5PI for other furnished lets. Enter total receipts, including charges billed to the tenant; the allowance is applied automatically. Micro lets already charged social contributions by a social security body go on lines 5NJ to 5PL instead (2026 Brochure pratique, chapter on non-salaried income, linked in Sources). | Exact retained current LF149/150/152 / 4.1 Micro-BIC |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 | 148 | - **Above the limit**, the réel regime applies (4.2). A taxpayer within the limit may also choose the réel regime. | Exact retained current LF149/150/152 / 4.1 Micro-BIC |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 | 149 | - **Micro-entrepreneur status is a different system.** A furnished let run as a micro-enterprise pays social contributions to Urssaf on turnover; see `fr-social-contributions`. Do not mix the micro-BIC income tax allowance with the micro-social rates. | Exact retained current LF153, 4.1 Micro-BIC |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 | 153 | - **Result.** Taxable profit = receipts minus deductible charges minus depreciation (amortissement), computed on form 2031 with the 2033 schedules and filed with the business tax office (SIE) of the property, or, with several properties, the SIE of the main home or of the largest furnished property (impots.gouv.fr page linked in 4.1). The profit goes to form 2042-C-PRO box 5NA, 5OA or 5PA; a deficit to 5NY, 5OY or 5PY. | FR-A18current2033referenceslimitedtoactualsimplified-realregime,2031withapplicableschedulespreserved. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 | 154 | - **Depreciation cannot create a loss.** BOFiP BOI-BIC-CHAMP-40-20 (§ 57, linked in Sources) says that under CGI art. 39 C, for an individual letting furnished property, the depreciation of the building and the furniture is deductible in a year only "dans la limite du montant du loyer acquis, diminué du montant des autres charges afférentes à ces biens". For depreciation over that limit, the page refers to BOI-BIC-AMT-20-40-10, which this skill has not read; do not assume it is lost or that it is carried forward without reading it. So depreciation can bring the result down to nil, but a deficit can come only from charges other than depreciation. | FR-A17 current4.2now suppliesongoingbookeddepreciationcarryundersame39Cceiling; cessation/sale/differentpermanentdisallowanceboundariesremain. |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 | 155 | - **Land is never depreciated.** BOFiP § 58: land does not depreciate, and the landlord must split the value of the building from the value of the land on the balance sheet, on their own responsibility. | Exact retained current LF159, 4.2 Réel LMNP |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 | 156 | - **Depreciation rates and useful lives.** No allowed page read for this skill prints rates or lives for a furnished let (the legacy text gave ranges). Use the useful life of each component under the general BIC rules; see `france-bookkeeping`. | Exact retained current LF160, 4.2 Réel LMNP |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 | 157 | - **LMNP deficits.** A deficit of a non-professional furnished letting activity cannot go against global income (CGI art. 156 I 1° ter, BOFiP § 240). It can be set only against profits from the same activity over the following ten years, in the years in which the activity is not professional (BOFiP § 250; impots.gouv.fr page: "ne s’imputent que sur des revenus provenant de la même activité au cours des dix années suivantes"). Deficits built up while non-professional cannot be used against global income or LMP profits if the activity later becomes professional. | Exact retained current LF161/163 / 4.2 Réel LMNP |
| 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 | 158 | - **Selling an LMNP property.** The gain is a private property gain (BOFiP § 290), not a business gain. For sales made from 15 February 2025, depreciation deducted by a non-professional furnished landlord is added back into the acquisition price, under conditions (service-public.gouv.fr F10864: "les amortissements que vous avez pu déduire sont réintégrés dans le prix d'acquisition"). The holding period allowances then apply: full income tax exemption beyond the 22nd year and full social levy exemption beyond the 30th year (F10864). The gain computation is outside this skill: refer. | FR-A11 current4.2corrects oldagency“addedtoacquisitionprice”to150VBIIIcostREDUCTION/increasedgain, withspecifiedexceptions and existinggainreferral. |
